Method: 1) 对两种基于记忆的方法进行再评估，沿两个维度扩展评估范围：多次运行以量化方差，随机打乱任务以考察顺序效应。2) 通过人工检查智能体的记忆内容，提出任务与环境不充分描述（underspecification）导致脆弱性的假设。3) 通过将更详细的信息（评分细则、环境反馈）纳入记忆构建过程来验证该假设。

Result: 两个关键发现：(1) 在复杂环境和多步任务中，智能体评估本质上噪声很大，自我改进回路会进一步放大噪声；(2) 智能体的改进高度依赖任务顺序，默认顺序隐式构成了课程学习（implicit curriculum），是成功的前提条件。加入规范信息后虽能部分缩小性能差距，但仍有显著空白。

Conclusion: 工作呼吁：(1) 自我改进智能体的评估应采用更严格的协议，报告多次运行结果并在挑战性条件下进行压力测试；(2) 不充分描述问题的发现要求设计支持有效人类监督的系统与接口，防止智能体以不可预见的方式失败。

Abstract: Memory-based self-improving agents--those that learn from an online stream of tasks and improve over time by maintaining a textual memory bank--have shown great promise in recent literature. However, the reliability aspects of these methods have been critically overlooked. In this work, we conduct a comprehensive re-evaluation of two memory-based methods, broadening the scope of evaluation along two axes: (1) including multiple runs to quantify variance, and (2) randomly shuffling the tasks to investigate the effect of task order. Through these experiments, we make two observations that expose the fragility of current methods: First, agent evaluation is inherently noisy in complex environments and on multi-step tasks, and stacking a self-improving loop on top can further amplify this noise. Second, the agent's improvement is highly dependent on task order. Prior works often adopt default orderings that impose an implicit curriculum, acting as a hidden prerequisite for success.
  To better understand this fragility, we manually examine the agents' memory and hypothesize that task and environment underspecification contribute to this fragility. We validate this hypothesis by incorporating information that enables better specification, such as detailed rubrics and environment feedback, into the memory construction process. While this added information partially closes the performance degradation in previous experiments, significant gaps still remain, suggesting that other uncharacterized factors contribute to this fragility. Looking ahead, our work advocates for more rigorous evaluation protocols for self-improving agents by reporting results across multiple runs and stress-testing them under challenging conditions. Moreover, our findings on underspecification call for systems and interfaces that enable effective human oversight, preventing agents from failing in unforeseeable ways.

</details>


### [198] [Procedural Content Metageneration via Program Search and Continual Abstraction Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17947)
*Matthew Siper,Ahmed Khalifa,Julian Togelius*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究利用大语言模型通过变异和交叉直接演化完整的程序化内容生成器（而非单个关卡），并在Sokoban、Zelda、Dangerous Dave和Lode Runner四个游戏领域中进行验证。作者提出持续抽象发现（CAD）方法，从高适应度程序中提取可复用原语到运行专有的辅助模块中。通过2×2实验（是否使用CAD × 是否提供手写领域API）完成160次完整运行，CAD在所有八项领域与API组合比较中均提升了平均最终最佳适应度，证明发现可复用原语能改善内容生成器的演化程序搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型能够生成可执行程序，使得直接在程序化内容生成器层面进行搜索成为可能，而非仅搜索单个关卡。作者希望探索如何利用LLM从演化过程中提取可复用的构建模块，以提高程序化内容生成的搜索效率。

Method: 提出Continual Abstraction Discovery（CAD）方法，在演化过程中持续从高适应度程序中提取可复用原语，存入运行特有的辅助模块中。每次运行通过语言模型变异和交叉演化完整的Python生成器。实验采用2×2设计：是否启用CAD × 是否提供固定手写领域API，在四个游戏领域中进行至少10次50代运行，共160次完整运行。

Result: CAD在所有八个领域与API组合比较中均提高了平均最终最佳适应度。在CAD运行的实验中，学习到的库被大多数后续程序采用，并反复发现了验证（validation）、可达性（reachability）和结构性工具（structural utilities）等可复用原语。

Conclusion: 实验结果支持发现可复用原语能够改善演化程序搜索在内容生成器上的表现，CAD作为一种持续抽象发现机制是有效的。

Abstract: Large language models can generate executable programs, which makes it possible to search directly over procedural content generators rather than individual levels. We study this approach in Sokoban, Zelda, Dangerous Dave, and Lode Runner. Each run evolves complete Python generators through language-model mutation and crossover. We introduce Continual Abstraction Discovery, or CAD, which extracts reusable primitives from high-fitness programs into a run-specific helper module. A 2x2 experiment crosses CAD with access to a fixed hand-written domain API. The completed data set contains 160 complete runs, with at least ten 50-generation runs in every cell. CAD raises mean final best fitness in all eight domain and API comparisons. Across all CAD runs, learned libraries are adopted by most later programs and repeatedly rediscover validation, reachability, and structural utilities. These results support that discovering reusable primitives improves evolutionary program search for content generators.

</details>


### [199] [Towards Zero-Shot Task Transfer with Neurosymbolic World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17959)
*Isidoro Tamassia,Lennert De Smet,Giuseppe Marra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种全新的神经符号世界模型，将奖励预测与观测重建解耦，使奖励预测仅依赖于潜在状态中结构化、符号化的子集，从而实现对新奖励函数的零样本泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于模型强化学习方法学习神经世界模型，虽在潜在空间中规划能力强且无环境结构假设，但模型是任务依赖的：潜在表征不可解释且与训练任务绑定，难以泛化到新任务。因此需要一种能解耦任务因素、支持跨任务泛化的世界模型。

Method: 提出神经符号世界模型（NeuroSymbolic World Model），核心思想是将观测重建与奖励预测解耦：奖励预测仅依赖于潜在状态的符号化、结构化子集，而非整个潜在状态。通过这种解耦设计，模型可在相同符号状态空间上定义的新奖励函数上实现零样本自适应。

Result: 实验表明，所提出的神经符号世界模型在泛化性能上显著优于纯神经方法，能够在不进行额外环境交互的情况下适应新的奖励函数。

Conclusion: 神经符号世界模型通过在潜在空间中引入结构化符号组件，有效解决了神经世界模型的任务依赖性问题，展示了较好的跨任务泛化能力，同时也揭示了这类方法的主要优势与挑战。

Abstract: State-of-the-art model-based reinforcement learning methods learn neural world models that allow policy improvement by planning in a latent space, without assumptions on the structure of the underlying environment. While expressive, these models are generally task-dependent: they learn uninterpretable latent representations that are tied to the training task and thus hard to generalize to new tasks. In this work, we present a novel world model formulation where the reward prediction only depends on a subset of structured, symbolic components of the whole latent state. Decoupling observation reconstruction and reward prediction allows us to learn world models that can adapt zero-shot, i.e. without further environment interactions, to new reward functions defined over the same symbolic state space. We discuss the main advantages and challenges of learning these neurosymbolic world models and demonstrate the strong generalisation properties of our approach over purely neural methods.

</details>


### [200] [Can Large Language Models Explain Flight Safety Events? A Prior-Guided Semantic LLM-based Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18017)
*Lu Xu,Xu Li,Linjiang Zheng,Fan Li,Riquan Zhang,Jiaxing Shang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FlightLLM，一种基于先验引导语义的大语言模型方法，用于可解释的飞行安全分析。该方法通过特征工程、语义离散化、CatBoost先验引导、对比少样本学习和结构化提示注入领域知识，在704个真实A320航班数据集上实现了竞争力的分类性能并生成合理的因果解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 飞行安全分析不仅需要准确检测风险事件，更需要在飞行员操控行为层面清晰解释其根本原因。现有可解释AI技术（如特征重要性图）需要大量领域知识才能转化为操作上有意义的解释，而LLM虽擅长语言推理，但在该领域面临模态不一致、分类能力有限、微调数据稀缺和缺乏领域知识等挑战。

Method: 首先进行特征工程以解决模态不一致问题，结合统计描述符与物理意义的飞行指标；然后通过语义离散化模块将抽象数值模式转换为定性描述；引入CatBoost作为统计专家，将其预测结果注入提示作为先验引导；采用对比少样本学习策略弥补数据不足；最后设计结构化提示将航空领域知识嵌入推理过程。

Result: 在包含704个真实A320航班样本的数据集上（以硬着陆为目标事件），FlightLLM在保持竞争力的分类性能的同时，能够生成直接且合理的事件原因解释。

Conclusion: FlightLLM成功克服了LLM在飞行安全分析中的模态不一致、分类能力弱、数据稀缺和领域知识缺乏等关键挑战，实现了分类性能与可解释性的统一。

Abstract: Improving flight safety with flight data requires not only accurate detection of risk events, but more importantly, clear interpretation of their underlying causes at the level of pilot control behavior. Existing explainable AI techniques, such as feature importance maps, often require considerable domain knowledge to translate them into operationally meaningful explanations. Large Language Models (LLMs), which excel at language reasoning, bring a promising solution to this issue. However, applying LLMs in this domain presents key challenges such as modal inconsistency, limited classification ability, scarcity of task-specific data for fine-tuning, and lack of domain knowledge. To overcome these challenges, we propose FlightLLM, a prior-guided semantic LLM-based approach for interpretable flight safety analysis. Specifically, we first perform feature engineering to address modal inconsistency, combining statistical descriptors with physically meaningful flight indicators. This representation is further processed by a Semantic Discretization module, which converts abstract numerical patterns into qualitative descriptions that are more compatible with language reasoning. In addition, since LLMs are not inherently strong classifiers, CatBoost is incorporated as a statistical expert, and its prediction results are injected into the prompt as prior guidance. A contrastive few-shot learning strategy is further adopted to compensate for limited data. Finally, we design structured prompts to embed aviation-specific knowledge into the inference process. Using hard landing, a representative risk event with complex causal mechanisms, as an anchor point, we evaluate FlightLLM on a dataset of 704 real-world A320 flight samples. Experimental results show that the proposed approach achieves competitive classification performance while generating direct and reasonable explanations for event causes.

</details>


### [201] [StagedWorkspace: A Versioned Workspace for Knowledge-Work Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18050)
*Yining Hua,Hongbin Na,Yifan Zhou,Akshay Kalose,Cyrus Ayubcha,Levi Lian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出StagedWorkspace，一个面向知识工作Agent的版本化工作空间，将解析视图与审查差异绑定到原生文件的内容哈希，确保所有视图明确关联到特定版本的工作空间状态。实验表明双重解析/原生访问优于单一视图访问，显著提升多模型在OfficeQA Pro和APEX-Agents上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI Agent在执行知识工作（如编写代码、制作文档、编辑电子表格等）时，其搜索的解析视图、编辑的原生文件、审查的变更和提交的工件可能引用同一产品的不同版本，造成状态不一致问题。虽然编码Agent已通过仓库契约部分解决此问题，但PDF、电子表格、幻灯片等非代码格式缺乏类似显式契约。

Method: 提出StagedWorkspace版本化工作空间，将解析记录和审查差异与原生文件的内容哈希绑定，随文件变化同步更新。在固定测试环境的消融实验中，比较双重解析/原生访问与单一视图访问的差异，并添加审查轴消融实验验证差异可见性的影响。

Result: 双重解析/原生访问在所有测试模型上获得最高点估计；OfficeQA Pass@1提升8.3-12.1点，APEX平均评分提升4.7-9.2点。SW-AGENT在OfficeQA上达63.9%（Gemini 3.1 Pro），在APEX上达42.1（GPT-5.4 Nano），显著优于已发表的同模型分数29.3%和25.5。审查差异可见时，57项文件编辑任务得分更高。

Conclusion: 工作空间状态是知识工作Agent的一个关键实验变量，应被显式建模。研究鼓励基准测试将证据、分阶段编辑和提交工件作为显式状态转换来评分。

Abstract: AI agents increasingly perform knowledge work (i.e., produce and modify persistent digital artifacts such as code repositories, documents, spreadsheets, slides, reports), yet the parsed views they search, the native files they edit, the changes they review, and the artifacts they submit can refer to different versions of the same work product. We formulate this as a workspace-state contract: every view should be explicitly tied to a version of the evolving workspace state. Coding agents partly address this need through repository contracts for search, diffs, and tests, whereas an analogous contract is less explicit for PDFs, spreadsheets, slides, notebooks, and mixed-format project folders. We propose StagedWorkspace, a versioned workspace for knowledge-work agents. The workspace binds parsed records and review diffs to content hashes of the native files as they change. In fixed-harness ablations on OfficeQA Pro and APEX-Agents, dual parsed/native access has the highest point estimate for every tested model; relative to the more limiting single view, it improves OfficeQA Pass@1 by 8.3-12.1 points and APEX mean rubric score by 4.7-9.2 points. SW-AGENT scores 63.9% with Gemini 3.1 Pro on OfficeQA and 42.1 with GPT-5.4 Nano on APEX, compared with published same-model scores of 29.3% and 25.5, respectively. A paired review-axis ablation on 57 file-editing tasks further finds higher observed scores when diffs are visible. These results identify workspace state as an experimental variable in knowledge-work agents and motivate benchmarks that score evidence, staged edits, and submitted artifacts as explicit state transitions.

</details>


### [202] [HLSR: Hybrid Live Forecast Selective Dynamic Vehicle Rerouting for Real-Time Congestion Avoidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18056)
*Xiao Wang,Shun Ren Yang,Hui Nien Hung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HLSR是一个选择性混合实时-预测车辆重路由框架，针对城市交通拥堵问题，通过有限干预范围融合实时边缘速度与短时预测，实现高效的路由重规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市交通拥堵降低生产力、增加出行成本和排放。网络级实时最短路径重路由在仿真中效果显著，但假设几乎所有车辆在每个决策周期都被重新规划，这在实际中不现实。

Method: HLSR框架基于双阈值拥堵检测、校准上游选择和驾驶员定制出行时间预测，并引入接近车辆扩展、出行时间加权k最短路径生成，以及用于多成本路径分配的时域依赖混合实时-预测路段速度。

Result: （摘要未提供具体实验结果数据）

Conclusion: HLSR通过限制干预范围而非全网络重路由的方式，实现了选择性车辆重路由，在保持高效性的同时克服了全网络重路由不切实际的假设。

Abstract: Urban traffic congestion reduces productivity and increases travel cost and emissions. Network-wide live travel-time shortest-path rerouting can be highly effective in simulation, but assumes that essentially every on-road vehicle is replanned every decision period. We propose HLSR, a selective hybrid live--forecast vehicle rerouting framework that fuses live edge speeds with short-horizon forecasts under limited intervention scope. Building on dual-threshold congestion detection, calibrated upstream selection, and driver-tailored travel-time prediction, HLSR further introduces approaching-vehicle expansion, travel-time-weighted k-shortest-path generation, and a horizon-dependent hybrid live--forecast segment speed used in multi-cost route allocation.

</details>


### [203] [Delegation Asymmetry in Agentic Recommender Systems: Measuring Two-Sided Receptivity in Online Dating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18058)
*Daria Leshchikova,Valentina V. Kuskova,Dmitry Zaytsev,Valerii Klimov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过两项大规模调查（N=2,894和N=2,617，两种语言）考察用户对自主LLM代理通信的接受度。研究发现"发送意愿"与"接收意愿"是高度相关但可分离的构念（rho=0.92，Delta BIC=52），并揭示了系统的委托不对称性：部署自己的代理所需接受度远低于参与对方代理。随机配对反事实分析显示仅4-13%的有向二元组能结合代理部署与接收方参与，且存在显著的性别方向失衡。设计反事实分析量化了各种干预手段的杠杆作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主LLM代理在匹配平台上代表用户对话是一种新兴设计模式，但其可行性依赖于一个鲜少检验的前提：用户不仅要接受将对话委托给代理，还要接受接收来自他人的代理中介通信。理解这种"代理接收度"是代理系统能否落地的关键条件。

Method: 采用潜变量测量模型（基于等级反应模型和潜回归），通过对活跃约会平台用户的大规模调查数据（两种语言、两个独立样本）进行建模，并通过模型比较验证发送与接收意愿的结构分离性；进一步使用反事实分析量化随机配对下的交互可行性及设计干预的杠杆效应。

Result: 发送与接收意愿是两个相关的独立构念；存在系统性委托不对称（部署阈值-0.38 vs 参与阈值+0.32），部署倾向约为参与倾向的三倍；随机配对下仅4-13%的二元组可行；互惠要求可将交互量削减一半以上；按接收接受度路由代理联系可使每次接触参与度提高三倍（AUC 0.88，3.1x四分位提升），并在样本外验证中稳健。

Conclusion: 代理中介通信的可行性与参与者是否愿意接收代理密切相关，而不仅是发送方是否愿意部署代理。研究为智能代理推荐系统的设计提供了重要启示，包括信息披露、选择加入机制以及接受度感知的匹配算法。

Abstract: Autonomous LLM agents that converse on a user's behalf are an emerging design pattern in matching platforms, yet their viability depends on a condition rarely examined: users must accept not only delegating conversation to an agent, but also receiving agent-mediated communication from others. We study this condition using two large-scale surveys of active users of a major dating platform (N=2,894 on generative profile features; N=2,617 on autonomous conversational agents, fielded in two languages). We develop a latent-variable measurement model of agent receptivity based on graded response models with latent regression, and show via model comparison that willingness to send and willingness to receive agent communication are distinct constructs: highly correlated (rho=0.92) but separable (Delta BIC=52), with partial measurement invariance across languages. The model quantifies a systematic delegation asymmetry: deploying one's own agent requires far lower receptivity (threshold -0.38) than engaging a counterpart's agent (+0.32; full engagement +1.39), and mean deployment propensity exceeds engagement propensity roughly threefold. Under a random-pairing counterfactual derived from stated receptivity, only 4-13% of directed dyads combine agent deployment with receiver engagement, with a pronounced gender-directional imbalance. Design counterfactuals quantify the levers: a reciprocity requirement cuts interaction volume by half or more by excluding nearly two-thirds of would-be deployment, while routing agent contacts on receive receptivity triples per-contact engagement, a lift that survives out-of-sample validation with the target item held out (AUC 0.88, 3.1x quartile lift under respondent-level cross-validation). We discuss implications for agentic recommender design, including disclosure, opt-in mechanics, and receptivity-aware matchmaking.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [204] [Proactive Road Safety Intervention in Australia: Predicting Risky Driving Hotspots from Connected Vehicle Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16913)
*Adriana-Simona Mihăiţă,Clarence Cheung,Artur Grigorev,Tuo Mao,David Lillo-Trynes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用澳大利亚Greater Sydney地区的联网车辆遥测数据，通过g-force阈值（急刹车、急转弯、急加速）量化危险驾驶行为，在地方政府区域（LGA）层面检测和预测接近事故的高风险驾驶事件。研究对比了集成学习、深度学习和经典时间序列三类共八种预测模型，发现ARIMA模型取得最低MAE（162.21），与LSTM表现相当，且优于所有集成方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的道路安全监测是被动式的，依赖事故发生后的伤亡记录分析。在事故发生前主动识别高风险地点和危险驾驶行为是一个关键但尚未充分探索的挑战，现有研究缺乏利用联网车辆遥测数据进行主动风险预测的工作。

Method: 利用Greater Sydney的联网车辆遥测数据，通过g-force阈值（硬刹车>0.6g、急转弯>0.47g、急加速>0.5g）量化危险驾驶，构建时空热力图识别高风险区域。对比八种预测模型，涵盖三类方法：集成学习（随机森林、XGBoost、LightGBM）、深度学习（LSTM、N-BEATS）和经典时间序列（ARIMA、指数平滑、Prophet），在LGA层面进行预测。

Result: ARIMA取得最低MAE（162.21），与LSTM（MAE: 163.92）表现相当，优于所有集成方法；N-BEATS的MAE为180.75。悉尼内城区和西部LGA（CBD、Parramatta、Bankstown）被识别为持续高风险区域。

Conclusion: 在训练数据量有限的情况下，简约的时间序列模型与深度学习方法具有竞争力。研究展示了基于IoT的联网车辆数据支持主动道路安全干预的潜力，并建议对悉尼高风险LGA区域采取针对性政策行动。

Abstract: Road safety monitoring has historically been reactive, relying on crash-record analysis after fatalities and injuries have already occurred. Proactive identification of high-risk locations and dangerous driving behaviour before incidents occur is a critical but underexplored challenge. This paper addresses this gap using connected vehicle telemetry data from Greater Sydney, Australia, to detect and forecast near-miss risky driving events at the Local Government Area (LGA) level. Risky driving is quantified through g-force thresholds (hard braking >0.6g, harsh cornering >0.47g, harsh acceleration >0.5g), and spatio-temporal heatmaps are constructed to identify high-risk zones. Eight predictive models are benchmarked across three families: ensemble learning (Random Forests, XGBoost, LightGBM), deep learning (LSTM, N-BEATS), and classical time-series methods (ARIMA, Exponential Smoothing, Prophet). ARIMA achieves the lowest mean absolute error (MAE: 162.21), performing comparably to LSTM (MAE: 163.92) and outperforming all ensemble methods, with N-BEATS reaching an MAE of 180.75. These results demonstrate that parsimonious time-series models are competitive with deep learning approaches when training data volume is limited. The study highlights the potential of IoT-based connected vehicle data to support proactive road safety interventions, with Sydney's inner and western LGAs (CBD, Parramatta, Bankstown) identified as persistent high-risk zones warranting targeted policy action.

</details>


### [205] [Detecting and Discriminating Operator Misspecification in Hybrid PDE-Parameter Learning: a Reference-Free Instrument, with Discrimination Bounded In Sample](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16925)
*Eric Fock*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了一种无需先验（oracle）即可从单次拟合中区分两类失效模式的工具：一是混合PDE参数估计器所假设的算子是否错误，二是参数是否仅不可识别。通过信息矩阵统计量与秩统计量，在正确指定、错误指定和不可识别三种设计下均能有效分离这两种失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的准确性检验（如RMSE）对估计器失效是"盲"的——即使系数误差高达29.7%，域内RMSE仍低于观测噪声，无法察觉错误。且现有的误设定检测与不可识别性检测混淆在一起，无法区分"算子假设错误"和"参数不可识别"这两种根本不同的失败原因。

Method: 提出一个基于信息矩阵的统计量（含plug-in尺度与每种子参数），从单次拟合中读取算子是否误设；同时结合秩统计量在不可识别设计下归零的特性，实现对两类失效的分离判别。并对比多种估计器（曲线拟合、裸参数、多层感知机、物理信息网络）验证其普遍性。

Result: 正确设定下中位数0.19，拒绝率0.033（低于预注册上限0.10）；两种错误设定下统计量分别升至224和85，全部样本触发报警。不可识别但正确设定的设计中保持静默（0.050），而秩统计量在边界c*5=2.15×10⁻³处归零。RMSE均远低于噪声水平，证明传统检验失效。

Conclusion: 该工具的核心贡献在于"分离"两种失效模式而非仅仅"检测"错误——在样本内是界，样本外是方向。同时揭示了物理信息网络因其复合目标函数会收敛到与标准估计器不同的伪真值，并报告了工具的盲区和预注册的负面结果。

Abstract: We build an instrument that reads, from a single fit and with no oracle, whether the operator a hybrid PDE-parameter estimator postulates is wrong-and separates that from a merely unidentifiable parameter. On one self-adjoint parabolic inverse problem, an information-matrix statistic with plug-in scale and per-seed parameter has median 0.19 under correct specification, rejection rate $0.033$ against a pre-registered ceiling of $0.10$, and rises to $224$ and $85$ under two misspecifications, firing in every replicate. On a correctly specified but non-identifiable design it stays mute-$0.050$ at $n=200$, Clopper-Pearson $[0.024, 0.090]$-while a rank statistic collapses to zero at a pre-registered boundary $c_5^*=2.15\times10^{-3}.$ Two readings of one fit therefore separate the two failures across the three designs a deployable test reaches. That separation is the contribution; detection alone is a crowded flank. In sample it is a bound, out of sample a direction. It is needed because the usual accuracy check is blind: the misspecified estimator's in-domain RMSE is $2.7\times 10^{-2}$, below the observation noise for $σ\geq 0.05,$ while the coefficient is wrong by $29.7\%$ at zero noise, $31.2\%$ at the loudest. Nor is the failure architectural: a one-parameter curve fit, a bare parameter and multilayer perceptrons of $49$ and $241$ parameters converge to the same pseudo-true, matched in closed form to $0.07\%,$ whereas a physics-informed network, with its composite objective, converges to a disjoint one. We report where the instrument is blind, a pre-registered negative where a neural estimator loses to Tikhonov-regularized inversion at recovery, and the hypothesis under which its guarantee holds but a trained network violates it.

</details>


### [206] [Data-DPO: Direct Preference Optimization for Target Model Data Selection in LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16926)
*Peng Sun,Yi Yang,Antong Zhang,Chunxiao Li,Yanbo Wang,Dianbo Liu,xin chen,Kai Yu,Lu Chen,Tianfan Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Data-DPO，一种面向目标模型的SFT数据选择方法。它通过一步探针观察目标模型在不同样本上的局部训练反馈，将样本间激活差异转化为成对数据偏好，训练轻量级奖励模型来学习目标模型感知的数据偏好。最终选择阶段结合目标模型偏好、外部质量分数和边际多样性来构建训练子集。实验表明在Vision-Flan和LLaVA-CoT上优于现有基线，并稳定超越全量数据训练性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据选择方法通常将数据价值视为相对静态的属性，忽视了数据与目标模型能力分布之间的兼容性。即同样的数据对不同能力的模型可能有不同的价值，需要面向特定目标模型进行数据选择。

Method: 1) 通过一步探针观察目标模型在不同样本上的局部训练反馈；2) 将样本间激活差异转化为成对数据偏好；3) 训练轻量级奖励模型学习目标模型感知的数据偏好；4) 最终选择结合目标模型偏好、外部质量分数和边际多样性构建训练子集。

Result: 在Vision-Flan和LLaVA-CoT数据集上，Data-DPO在多种数据预算下一致优于现有数据选择基线，并稳定超越全量数据训练的性能。

Conclusion: Data-DPO证明了将数据选择与目标模型能力分布对齐的有效性，通过目标模型感知的数据偏好实现了更高效、更稳定的SFT数据选择。

Abstract: Data selection in supervised fine-tuning aims to select a small set of effective samples from large-scale candidate data, reducing training cost while preserving model performance. However, existing methods usually treat data value as a relatively static property, and pay limited attention to the compatibility between data and the capability distribution of the target model. To address this issue, we propose Data-DPO, a target model-oriented SFT data selection method. Data-DPO observes the local training feedback of the target model on different samples through one-step probing, transforms activation differences among samples into pairwise data preferences, and trains a lightweight reward model to learn target-model-aware data preferences. In the final selection stage, Data-DPO further combines target model preference, external quality scores, and marginal diversity to construct a more stable and effective training subset. Experimental results on Vision-Flan and LLaVA-CoT show that Data-DPO consistently outperforms existing data selection baselines under multiple data budgets and stably surpasses full data training performance.

</details>


### [207] [Hierarchical Data Selection via Manifold Coverage and Sparse Feature Coverage in LLM Post-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16927)
*Peng Sun,Yi Yang,Antong Zhang,Chunxiao Li,Yanbo Wang,Dianbo Liu,xin chen,Kai Yu,Lu Chen,Tianfan Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MASS方法，将数据选择任务建模为从粗到细的层次覆盖问题，通过密集自编码器学习低维主流形坐标进行粗粒度语义分组，再利用TopK稀疏自编码器在组内进行质量感知的稀疏特征覆盖，从而在大型候选中挑选高价值数据子集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的数据选择方法直接在原始嵌入空间中度量多样性，但该空间中几何度量会纠缠主导语义方向、细粒度监督差异和局部噪声，导致难以准确选择高价值数据子集。

Method: 提出MASS方法，包含两个阶段：1) 使用密集自编码器学习低维主流形坐标，实现粗粒度的语义分组；2) 在每个组内使用TopK稀疏自编码器进行质量感知的稀疏特征覆盖，实现细粒度的数据选择。

Result: 在Vision Flan和LLaVA-CoT两个基准上，MASS在多种预算设置下持续优于强数据选择基线，在若干场景下仅用少量数据子集即可匹配甚至超越全量数据训练的效果。

Conclusion: MASS通过从粗到细的层次覆盖策略，有效解决了嵌入空间中几何度量混淆问题，在降低训练成本的同时提升了模型性能，证明了层次化稀疏覆盖在数据选择任务中的有效性。

Abstract: As supervised fine-tuning data continues to scale, selecting high-value subsets from large candidate pools is crucial for reducing training cost and improving model performance. Existing methods often measure diversity directly in the original embedding space, where geometric metrics entangle dominant semantic directions, fine-grained supervision differences, and local noise. We address this limitation by formulating data selection as a coarse-to-fine hierarchical coverage problem and propose MASS. MASS learns low-dimensional principal manifold coordinates with a dense autoencoder for coarse semantic grouping, and then performs quality-aware sparse feature coverage within each group using a TopK sparse autoencoder. Experiments on Vision Flan and LLaVA-CoT show that MASS consistently outperforms strong data selection baselines across multiple budgets, and in several settings matches or surpasses full data training with only a small subset of data.

</details>


### [208] [Benchmarking Classical and Transformer-Based Models for Document Sensitivity Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16928)
*Aleesha Zainab,Muhammad Ahmed Khalid,Faheem Ullah Khan,Asifullah Khan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Strategic 16K——一个基于WikiLeaks PlusD外交电报的泄漏控制数据集（16,000条），并系统性地解决了自动敏感度分类中标签泄漏（label leakage）问题。作者建立了去除三类残留分类标记的泄漏清除协议，并对六种模型架构（从经典机器学习到Transformer）进行了基准评测。结果显示BERT表现最佳（Accuracy=89.14%, F1=89.33%），TF-IDF+逻辑回归在较低计算成本下表现优异，这是首个在明确泄漏控制条件下从WikiLeaks PlusD构建的可复现敏感度分类基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动敏感度分类是一个关键但未受充分重视的问题，误分类可能导致监管违规或安全漏洞。然而，该领域存在一个普遍但未被充分报道的问题——标签泄漏（label leakage），即文档中残留的分类标记使得模型利用表面捷径而非真正的内容信号进行分类，导致性能估计虚高且不可靠，从而影响模型的真实可靠性和安全性。

Method: 1) 从WikiLeaks PlusD构建了Strategic 16K数据集（16,000条外交电报）；2) 设计了一套扩展的泄漏清除协议，识别并消除三类嵌入文档正文中的残留分类标记；3) 在清除泄漏后的干净基准上系统评估六种模型架构，涵盖经典机器学习（如TF-IDF+逻辑回归）和Transformer方法（如BERT、ELECTRA等）。

Result: 在干净的基准上，BERT表现最佳（Accuracy=89.14%, F1=89.33%），ELECTRA次之（Accuracy=88.57%, F1=88.90%）。在经典模型中，TF-IDF+逻辑回归在显著更低的计算成本下达到最强性能。这是首个在明确的泄漏控制条件下从WikiLeaks PlusD构建的完全可复现的敏感度分类基准。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了自动敏感度分类中标签泄漏问题的严重性，提供了一种系统性的泄漏清除方法，并给出了可复现的基准结果，为该领域的后续研究奠定了数据和方法基础。同时表明Transformer模型在泄漏控制条件下仍能取得良好性能，且经典模型在计算效率和性能之间提供了务实的选择。

Abstract: Automatic sensitivity classification of organizational documents is a critical yet underserved problem, where the consequences of misclassification range from regulatory violations to security breaches. While AI-based approaches offer a scalable alternative to manual review, their reliability depends fundamentally on the integrity of training data. A pervasive but underreported problem in this domain is label leakage: residual classification markers embedded within document bodies that allow models to exploit surface shortcuts rather than learning genuine content-based sensitivity signals, producing performance estimates that are inflated and unreliable. This paper addresses this problem by introducing Strategic 16K, a carefully constructed, leakage-controlled corpus of 16,000 diplomatic cables sourced from the WikiLeaks Public Library of US Diplomacy (PlusD), and presents a systematic benchmark evaluating six model architectures spanning classical machine learning and transformer-based approaches. We document an extended leakage removal protocol that identifies and eliminates three categories of residual classification markers embedded within document bodies. On the clean benchmark, BERT achieves the strongest performance (Accuracy = 89.14%, F1 = 89.33%), followed by ELECTRA (Accuracy = 88.57%, F1 = 88.90%). Among classical models, TF-IDF with Logistic Regression achieves the strongest performance at significantly lower computational cost. These results constitute the first fully reproducible sensitivity classification benchmark constructed under explicit leakage-controlled conditions from WikiLeaks PlusD.

</details>


### [209] [Mr.Dec: Daily-Scale Longitudinal Multimodal Modeling for 30-Day Readmission Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16929)
*Minjun Kim,Jong Hak Moon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Mr.Dec（多模态再入院风险预测解码器），将每次住院建模为日常多模态事件的自然时间序列，利用Transformer解码器整合每日EHR更新和间歇性胸片发现，并通过疾病特定监督对比学习作为辅助正则化，在MIMIC-IV和MIMIC-CXR数据集上实现最先进性能，同时识别住院期间的"关键日"以提供可解释的实时风险分层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法往往将复杂的纵向住院历史压缩为固定表示，丢失了反映患者生理状态演变的每日粒度临床信号。由于临床风险随住院期间证据积累而动态变化，捕获这些动态轨迹至关重要。

Method: 将每次住院建模为每日多模态事件的自然时间序列；利用Transformer解码器将每日EHR更新与间歇性CXR发现整合为时间对齐的流；采用疾病特定监督对比学习作为辅助正则化，在潜在空间中诱导诊断感知结构。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV和MIMIC-CXR数据集上的评估表明，Mr.Dec通过保留临床序列的完整性实现了最先进的性能；此外，该模型能够识别住院期间的"关键日"，提供可操作且临床可解释的实时风险分层依据。

Conclusion: Mr.Dec通过保留每日粒度的时间序列信息和跨模态整合，验证了动态建模住院过程对30天再入院预测的有效性，同时通过"关键日"识别增强了模型的临床可解释性和实用性。

Abstract: Predicting 30-day hospital readmission is essential for assessing patient stability and optimizing healthcare resources. As clinical risk evolves with the accumulation of evidence during hospitalization, capturing these dynamic trajectories is essential. However, many existing approaches compress the complex longitudinal history into fixed representations, often losing the granular, day-level clinical signals that reflect a patient's evolving physiological state. To address this, we propose Mr.Dec (Multimodal Readmission-risk prediction Decoder), which models each admission as a natural chronological sequence of daily multimodal events. By leveraging a Transformer Decoder, Mr.Dec integrates daily Electronic Health Record(EHR) updates and intermittent Chest X-ray(CXR) findings in a time-aligned stream, reflecting the actual clinical workflow. To ensure robustness, we utilize Disease-Specific Supervised Contrastive Learning as an auxiliary regularization to induce a diagnosis-aware structure in the latent space. Evaluations on the MIMIC-IV and MIMIC-CXR datasets show that Mr.Dec achieves state-of-the-art performance by preserving the integrity of the clinical sequence. Furthermore, our model identifies "Critical Days" within an admission, providing actionable and clinically grounded interpretations for real-time risk stratification. Code is available at: https://github.com/yejix-ai/MR.DEC

</details>


### [210] [EMAN: Optimization-Driven Capacity Growth through Path Emergence in Multi-Task Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16930)
*Chenlei Fang,Jingchen Li,Hongzong LI,Qingyao Li,Yixuan Zhang,Huarui Wu,Haobin Shi,Chunjiang Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Emergent Modular Atomic Network（EMAN），一种优化驱动的多任务学习框架，网络从单路径计算开始，仅当持续优化证据出现时才动态生长出新的独立路径，实现共享与任务特定表示能力的自适应分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MTL方法（硬共享、多路径/专家、自适应共享、动态扩展）的容量变化受限于预定义结构或由任务边界/冲突信号触发。作者提出疑问：网络能否从精确的单路径计算出发，仅在持续优化证据出现时才生长独立路径，实现更灵活的容量分配。

Method: EMAN通过潜在相对相位暴露反对称生长方向（无需实例化第二路径），在训练期间监控多个决策信号以将局部优化证据转化为结构决策。仅在认证后才实例化两条等容量独立路径，并自适应分配共享与任务特定表示容量。

Result: 在受控秩设置、PASCAL-Context和NYUv2上的广泛实验验证了其有效性，在具有竞争力的计算成本下实现更好的性能。

Conclusion: EMAN证明基于优化证据驱动的动态结构生长是可行的，能以竞争性计算成本提升多任务学习性能，为超越预定义结构和边界触发的MTL容量分配提供了新范式。

Abstract: Existing multi-task learning methods rely on hard sharing, multiple paths or experts, adaptive sharing, and dynamic expansion. However, their capacity changes are usually constrained by predefined structures or triggered by task boundaries and conflict signals. This raises a fundamental question: can a network start from exact single-path computation and grow a new independent path only when persistent optimization evidence appears? We propose the Emergent Modular Atomic Network (EMAN), an optimization-driven framework for exposing an antisymmetric growth direction through latent relative phases without instantiating a second path, and for monitoring multiple decision signals during training to transform local optimization evidence into a structural decision. EMAN materializes two equal-capacity independent paths only after certification. EMAN adaptively allocates shared and task-specific representation capacity to accommodate varying task requirements. Extensive experiments on controlled rank settings, PASCAL-Context, and NYUv2 validate its effectiveness, achieving improved performance at a competitive computational cost.

</details>


### [211] [SW-ProxyCE: Zero-Query Adversarial Transfer from Public EEG Encoders to Private Downstream Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16931)
*Linhua Cong,Dingkun Liu,Dongrui Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了EEG基础模型在"公开编码器+私有下游模型"部署场景下的对抗迁移攻击风险，提出SW-ProxyCE框架，通过收缩白化类原型从少量标记参考集恢复任务级决策几何，实现免查询的迁移对抗攻击，实验证明该攻击能有效迁移至不可访问的下游模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG基础模型编码器的公开释放带来下游开发便利的同时，引入了一个此前未被探索的安全风险——公开可用的表示可能使私有下游模型变得脆弱。现有研究缺乏对EEG基础模型部署场景下对抗迁移攻击的系统性分析。

Method: 提出SW-ProxyCE (Shrinkage-Whitened Proxy Cross-Entropy)框架：攻击者具有公开编码器的白盒访问权限和少量任务匹配的标记参考集，通过对类原型进行收缩白化处理来恢复任务的决策几何信息，无需训练额外的替代分类器即可生成可迁移的对抗样本。

Result: 在三个EEG任务、三种通用基础编码器和一个范式特定预训练编码器上评估，覆盖线性探测和全微调下游模型，跨被试和被试内场景。结果显示从公开编码器和有限标记参考生成的对抗样本能有效迁移到不可访问的下游模型，SW-ProxyCE一致优于任务无关的表示偏移攻击。

Conclusion: EEG基础模型的强迁移性并不必然带来对抗鲁棒性，公开编码器部署模式存在显著的安全漏洞，需要在公开发布时考虑相应的防御策略。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) foundation models have recently emerged as a promising paradigm for EEG decoding by learning reusable representations from large-scale heterogeneous neural recordings. However, the open release of EEG foundation encoders, while facilitating downstream developments, also introduces a previously unexplored security risk: publicly available representations may make private downstream models vulnerable. This paper investigates adversarial transfer attacks in EEG foundation model deployment in a public-encoder and private-downstream setting, where attackers have white-box access to a released encoder and a small task-matched labeled reference set, but no access or query to victim parameters, outputs, or gradients. We propose Shrinkage-Whitened Proxy Cross-Entropy (SW-ProxyCE), a query-free task-aware attack framework that recovers task-level decision geometry from a small labeled reference set through shrinkage-whitened class prototypes, enabling transferable adversarial generation without training an additional surrogate classifier. We evaluated SW-ProxyCE across three EEG tasks using three general-purpose foundation encoders and a paradigm-specific pre-trained encoder, covering both linear-probing and full-fine-tuning downstream models in cross-subject and within-subject scenarios. Results demonstrated that adversarial examples generated from the public encoder and limited labeled references can effectively transfer to inaccessible downstream models. SW-ProxyCE consistently outperformed task-agnostic representation-shift attacks, revealing that the strong transferability of EEG foundation models does not necessarily lead to adversarial robustness. Our code will be available on GitHub.

</details>


### [212] [DOW-KE: Anchor-Free Multi-Layer Knowledge Editing via Direct End-to-End Weight Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16932)
*Ran Chen,Junbo Zhang,Qianli Zhou,Xinyang Deng,Wen Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DOW-KE是一种无锚点的知识编辑方法，核心原则是"优化什么就必须部署什么"。它通过反向传播最终编辑目标到完整模型，联合优化所有编辑层的权重更新，使跨层传播和耦合进入每个梯度步骤，并将保留投影嵌入更新参数化中，从而弥合锚点目标与实际编辑之间的闭合差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多层定位-编辑（locate-then-edit）方法存在闭合差距（closure gap）：它们先优化中间表示（锚点），再通过局部求解逐层部署权重更新，但联合的权重更新在真实前向传播中的效果从未被优化，导致传播引起的衰减和失真无法被校正。

Method: DOW-KE采用无锚点设计，直接通过完整模型反向传播最终编辑目标，联合优化所有编辑层的更新参数。保留机制嵌入在更新参数化内部（计算图中），使每个梯度都作用于实际部署的更新，而非事后添加约束。

Result: 在两个数据集和三个模型上的大规模顺序编辑实验中，DOW-KE在六种模型-数据集组合中的五种中获得最高的总体Score和邻域特异性（Specificity），优于所有评估的基线方法。

Conclusion: DOW-KE验证了"优化与部署一致性"原则在知识编辑中的有效性，通过联合优化和内生保留投影，同时实现了高编辑准确性和对受保护知识的良好保持。

Abstract: Multi-layer locate-then-edit methods for knowledge editing first optimize target residual-stream activations (anchors) at selected layers, then realize them layer by layer as weight updates. This pipeline optimizes an intermediate representation but deploys multi-layer weight updates whose joint effect through the true forward pass is never itself optimized: regardless of how anchors are set or propagated, each update comes from a local solve, so propagation-induced attenuation and distortion go uncorrected, leaving a closure gap between anchor targets and realized edits. We propose DOW-KE, an anchor-free method built on a single principle: what is optimized must be exactly what is deployed. DOW-KE backpropagates the final editing objective through the complete model, jointly optimizing the updates of all edited layers so cross-layer propagation and coupling enter every gradient step. The same principle dictates where preservation resides: embedding the preservation projection in the update parameterization, inside the computation graph, makes every gradient act on the deployed update; post-hoc constraints would reopen the gap, and the constrained search keeps edits clear of protected knowledge. In large-scale sequential editing on two datasets and three models, DOW-KE achieves the highest overall Score and neighborhood Specificity in five of six model-dataset settings among the evaluated baselines.

</details>


### [213] [Study-Strategy Clusters from EdNet Logs Track Engagement, Not Mastery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16963)
*Qingchuan Lyu,Yingxin Li,Albert Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究在EdNet-KT3数据集上对5000名活跃学习者的智能辅导系统（ITS）日志进行聚类分析，发现行为聚类能描述学习风格和参与度（如持久性η²≈0.106），但无法预测无辅助的后期准确性（p_adj≈0.093），且与知识追踪模型的掌握信号几乎无关（ARI=0.007）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习分析领域常常默认将无监督聚类出的学习者类型视为能够预测学习效果的因素，但这一假设缺乏充分验证。作者希望检验这一核心假设是否成立。

Method: 对5000名学习者的学习策略特征（资源使用、复习、视频、问题练习）进行聚类，采用轮廓系数选取k=5的父切分，识别出4个对比极（阅读型、视频型、复习型、问题型）及一个近均值残差类；对残差再聚类得到8种稳定层次风格；按答题数分割时间线，用前半段聚类预测后半段结果；并使用知识追踪模型SAKT对比行为风格。

Result: 早期聚类能预测后期参与度（持久性η²≈0.106，完成度η²≈0.021），但无法预测无辅助准确性（p_adj≈0.093）；纯音量聚类与策略标签匹配度低（ARI=0.064）；SAKT的掌握信号与行为风格几乎独立（ARI=0.007）。

Conclusion: 行为聚类能够描述学习风格与参与度，但并不能反映知识获取或学习效果，因此将无监督聚类作为学习者学习效果预测因子的假设需要谨慎对待。

Abstract: Learning analytics often treats unsupervised clusters of intelligent tutoring system (ITS) logs as learner types that should predict learning. We test that assumption on EdNet-KT3. Clustering study-strategy features (resource use, revision, video, problem practice) for 5{,}000 active learners yields a silhouette-selected parent cut ($k=5$) with 4 contrast poles (reading-focused, video-heavy, revision-heavy, and problem-first) plus a large near-mean residual ($\sim$64.9\%). Reclustering that residual adds four finer styles, giving a bootstrap-stable hierarchy of 8 named strategies. We split each learner's timeline by respond count so clusters use only the early half and outcomes only the late half. Early clusters predict later engagement (continuing to practice and finishing late sessions, especially persistence, $η^{2}\approx 0.106$; completion $η^{2}\approx 0.021$) but not later unassisted accuracy (correctness on late first-attempts without help; $p_{\mathrm{adj}}\approx 0.093$). Volume rises with some styles, yet volume-only clustering barely matches strategy labels (ARI$=0.064$). A knowledge-tracing model (SAKT) on the seven TOEIC exam sections predicts next correctness only modestly better than a baseline that knows only how hard each section usually is (AUC lift $+0.051$; CI $[+0.045,+0.058]$), and that mastery signal is nearly independent of behavior styles (ARI$=0.007$). Behavioral clustering here describes study styles and engagement, not knowledge gains.

</details>


### [214] [RoBell-RVFL: A Robust Generalized Bell Random Vector Functional Link Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16965)
*A. Rahaman,A. Quadir,M. Tanveer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RoBell-RVFL，一种鲁棒且轻量的质量感知广义钟形随机向量功能链接网络，通过双策略样本级加权机制（少数类单位权重保护 + 多数类概率加权gbell隶属函数抑制噪声/边界/离群样本）在核诱导特征空间中处理类不平衡和标签噪声，在UCI和KEEL基准及高达40%标签噪声下显著优于现有RVFL变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实数据集中多数类的支配地位会使随机神经网络产生决策边界偏差，忽视关键少数类样本。现有方法（如SMOTE和类加权损失）主要关注类比例而忽略类内分布，在标签噪声和离群值面前脆弱。作者旨在解决随机模型在类不平衡和噪声数据下的鲁棒性问题。

Method: RoBell-RVFL采用双策略样本级加权机制：1) 对少数类样本施加单位权重以严格保留其信息；2) 对多数类样本通过核诱导特征空间中的概率加权广义钟形（gbell）隶属函数自适应调节其影响。该机制显式融入局部类别概率和类分布信息，抑制多数类中的噪声、边界和离群样本，同时保持RVFL网络的闭式学习效率。

Result: 在UCI和KEEL基准数据集上的广泛评估以及高达40%标签噪声下的鲁棒性测试表明，RoBell-RVFL一致且显著优于最新最先进的RVFL变体。

Conclusion: 自适应、质量感知的样本加权对于鲁棒RVFL学习至关重要，传统的全局加权方案在噪声和不平衡环境中效果不佳。

Abstract: The dominance of majority classes in real-world datasets poses a fundamental challenge to randomized neural networks, often biasing decision boundaries and overlooking critical minority samples. Existing remedies, such as synthetic minority over-sampling (SMOTE) and class-weighted loss functions, primarily address class proportions while neglecting intra-class distribution, making them vulnerable to label noise and outliers. In this paper, we propose \textbf{RoBell-RVFL}, a robust and lightweight \emph{quality-aware} generalized bell random vector functional link network that redefines how randomized models handle class imbalance and noisy data. RoBell-RVFL employs a dual-strategy, sample-level weighting mechanism that strictly preserves minority class information using unit weights, while adaptively regulating the influence of majority class samples through a probability-weighted generalized bell (gbell) membership function in a kernel-induced feature space. This design effectively suppresses noisy, boundary, and outlier samples within the majority class, enabling the network to learn from informative samples rather than merely abundant ones. By explicitly incorporating local class probability and class distribution information into the learning process, RoBell-RVFL achieves adaptive control over sample contributions without sacrificing the closed-form learning efficiency of RVFL networks. Extensive evaluations on UCI and KEEL benchmark datasets, along with robustness tests under up to 40\% label noise, demonstrate that RoBell-RVFL consistently and significantly outperforms recent state-of-the-art RVFL variants. The results indicate that adaptive, quality-aware sample weighting is essential for robust RVFL learning, rendering conventional global weighting schemes ineffective in noisy and imbalanced environments.

</details>


### [215] [MultiSigBERT: Beyond Survival Analysis through Multimodal and Sequential Modeling in Oncology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16972)
*Paul Minchella,Stéphane Chrétien,Guillaume Metzler,Loïc Verlingue,Rémi Vaucher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MultiSigBERT，一个基于路径签名表示的多模态时序生存分析统一框架，结合EHR中的自由文本和结构化数据，并通过LASSO-Cox模型实现个体化风险预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代医疗中，EHR包含互补的多模态信息（临床叙述文本、数值测量和结构化变量），但大多数生存模型局限于单一模态，且未能充分利用患者轨迹的时序特性。

Method: 将医学文本通过BERT提取并平均上下文词嵌入转换为句子嵌入，经PCA压缩后与结构化协变量拼接形成联合时序轨迹；利用Rough Paths理论中的Signature变换高效捕获跨模态高阶时序交互；最终将Signature特征输入LASSO正则化的Cox模型进行风险评分。

Result: 在Léon Bérard中心真实肿瘤队列（超2500名患者、12万份医疗报告）上，独立测试集实现一致性指数（C-index）为0.743（标准差0.029），证明了多模态时序动态与患者级几何结构联合建模对生存预测的显著优势。

Conclusion: 联合建模多模态时序动态与患者级几何结构能显著提升生存预测性能，MultiSigBERT为肿瘤学临床决策支持提供了有效的统一框架。

Abstract: Machine learning has become an essential component of modern healthcare, where the integration of heterogeneous data sources offers unprecedented opportunities to improve clinical decision-making. Electronic Health Records (EHR) contain complementary information -- including narrative clinical reports, numerical measurements, and structured variables -- yet most survival models remain limited to a single modality or fail to exploit the temporal nature of patient trajectories. We propose MultiSigBERT, a unified framework for multimodal sequential survival modeling in oncology based on path signature representations. Here, narrative medical reports (free-text) are converted into sentence embeddings by extracting and averaging contextual word embeddings. These representations are then compressed via modality-specific PCA and concatenated with structured covariates to form joint temporal trajectories which are then encoded using the Signature transform, a tool from Rough Paths theory that efficiently captures higher-order temporal interactions across modalities without supervision needed. The computed Signature features are finally incorporated as high dimensional features into a LASSO-regularized Cox model to estimate individualized risk scores. The performance of our novel MultiSigBERT pipeline is illustrated on the analysis of a real-world oncology cohort from the Léon Bérard Center, comprising over 120,000 medical reports and structured records from more than 2,500 patients. The model achieves a concordance index of 0.743 (sd 0.029) on an independent test set, demonstrating the benefit of jointly modeling multimodal temporal dynamics together with patient-level geometric structure for survival prediction.

</details>


### [216] [Position: Fairness Failure in Generative Models is an Evaluation Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16974)
*Mariia Vladimirova,Jean-Yves Franceschi,Thibaut Issenhuth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是一篇立场论文，指出生成模型中的公平性失败本质上是评估问题：公平性研究结果难以跨论文比较或用于部署决策。作者提出Fairness Cards作为最小报告工件，使评估选择（提示族、反事实协议、指标和拒绝处理）显式化，从而实现可复现性、可比性和可问责性，并建议转向生成模型特有的标准化评估标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管生成模型在过去十年取得了突破性进展，但关于其缺乏公平性、强化社会不平等和伤害边缘化群体的问题仍未被充分解决且难以采取行动。作者认为公平性发现难以跨论文比较或用于部署决策，这从根本上是一个评估问题。

Method: 本文诊断了当前实践中反复出现的经验性和概念性失败模式，并提出Fairness Cards作为最小报告工件，明确记录评估选择（包括提示族、反事实协议、指标和拒绝处理），以支持可复现性、可比性和可问责性。

Result: 论文提议从临时性偏见检查转向标准化、生成模型特有的评估，引入了Fairness Cards这一报告工件，并提供了项目页面供进一步参考。

Conclusion: 作者呼吁评估标准的范式转变，并提出了额外的建议以推动这一转变，强调需要通过标准化评估工件实现公平性评估的可复现、可比和可问责。

Abstract: Despite groundbreaking advancements in generative models during the last decade, concerns about their lack of fairness, reinforcing societal inequalities and harming marginalized groups, remain under-addressed and difficult to act upon. This position paper argues that fairness failures in generative models, albeit driven by multiple factors, are ultimately stemming from an evaluation problem: fairness findings are rarely comparable across papers or actionable for deployment decisions. This paper diagnoses recurring empirical and conceptual failure modes in current practice and motivates a shift from ad-hoc bias checks to standardized, generative-specific evaluation. We propose Fairness Cards as a minimal reporting artifact that makes evaluation choices explicit (prompt families, counterfactual protocols, metrics, and refusal handling) enabling reproducibility, comparability, and accountability. We conclude with additional recommendations towards a paradigm shift in evaluation standards. Our project page can be found at https://mariiavladimirova.github.io/fairness-cards .

</details>


### [217] [Agents unlock new capabilities through Switching LoRA Adapters as a Tool (SLAaaT)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17034)
*Kenneth Ge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在长智能体轨迹中通过LoRA适配器切换工具来解决灾难性遗忘问题的方法，允许模型在任务执行过程中自主切换专业化适配器，相比单一适配器可减少高达18倍的能力损耗，并在任务能力和token使用上均显著优于子智能体方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后训练（post-training）能解锁新能力并提升特定任务表现，但常以其他领域的灾难性遗忘为代价。在需要组合多种能力的长时间智能体轨迹中，这种能力权衡成为严重问题，作者拒绝接受这一权衡，希望找到能同时保持多种专业能力的方案。

Method: 给智能体提供一个工具，使其能够在轨迹执行过程中（mid-trace）切换不同的专门LoRA适配器。作者组合了两个逻辑简单但需要专业化的合成编码任务来测试该方法的效果。

Result: 该方法使模型能够解决之前无法解决的问题；模型能自主切换适配器，并在一个任务上发现了优于人工启发式基线的全新策略；相比仅使用单一专门适配器的智能体，能力损耗（capability tax）最多减少18倍；在任务能力和token使用量上均大幅优于生成子智能体的方案。

Conclusion: 通过提供LoRA适配器切换工具，可以在不牺牲专业能力的前提下解决灾难性遗忘问题，是一种高效且资源节约的方案。

Abstract: Post-training can unlock new capabilities and improve performance on specialized tasks, but sometimes at the cost of catastrophic forgetting in other domains. This poses a problem in long agent trajectories that compose different capabilities. We reject this tradeoff by giving an agent a tool to switch between specialized LoRA adapters mid-trace. To test its effectiveness, we compose two synthetic coding tasks that are logically simple but require specialization. We find that this allows the model to solve problems it previously could not, that the model is able to switch autonomously (and find a new strategy that beats our human heuristic baseline on one task), and that this incurs an up to an 18x reduction in capability tax compared to an agent using only one specialized adapter. Our approach also substantially outperforms spawning subagents in both task capabilities and token usage.

</details>


### [218] [J-Miner: Recovering Executable Decision Knowledge from Language-Model Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17063)
*Yunfan Gao,Xinyi Huang,Tao Sheng,Haorui Song,Yun Xiong,Haofen Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出J-Miner方法，从微调后的大语言模型分类器中挖掘内部决策知识，将其编码为可检查、可验证、可复用的可执行规则表示。通过聚合跨层和词位置的词汇对齐内部信号来挖掘命名概念，并利用分类器自身预测学习可执行决策规则。实验表明J-Miner规则能复现高达98.3%的源分类器决策，行为保真度比输入词汇学习的规则高6.0-29.5个百分点，且可将决策知识迁移到参数少约24倍的轻量级学生模型中，同时保留源分类器99.8%的平均任务准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型微调而成的专用分类器虽然性能优异，但通常只输出最终标签，微调过程中获得的决策知识隐式存在于模型内部，难以检查、验证和复用。作者希望挖掘这种内部决策知识，并将其编码为显式可执行的表示形式。

Method: J-Miner包含两个核心步骤：(1)通过聚合跨层和跨词位置的词汇对齐内部信号，从微调分类器中挖掘文本级命名概念；(2)利用分类器自身的预测结果，在这些概念上学习可执行的决策规则，将局部内部读出蒸馏为显式的分类器级知识表示。

Result: 在多个分类任务上，J-Miner规则可复现高达98.3%的源分类器决策；与从输入词汇学习的同等紧凑规则相比，行为保真度高出6.0-29.5个百分点。命名概念反映与任务决策相关的内部语义证据，学习到的规则将分布式信号整合为可检查的决策结构。迁移到参数量约为源分类器1/24的轻量级学生模型时，可保留源分类器99.8%的平均任务准确率。

Conclusion: 任务特定的决策知识可以被忠实表示为显式、可执行的形式，并能在源分类器之外复用。这为解释、验证和迁移大语言模型内部决策知识提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Large language models can be fine-tuned into specialized classifiers that perform well across diverse text tasks and make complex judgments, but they typically expose only final labels, leaving the decision knowledge acquired through fine-tuning implicit within the model. We study how to mine this internal decision knowledge from a fine-tuned classifier and encode it in an executable representation that can be inspected, validated, and reused beyond the source classifier. We introduce J-Miner, which mines text-level named concepts by aggregating vocabulary-aligned internal signals across layers and token positions, and uses the classifier's own predictions to learn executable decision rules over them. This process distills local internal readouts into an explicit classifier-level knowledge representation. Across multiple classification tasks, J-Miner rules reproduce up to 98.3\% of source-classifier decisions and achieve 6.0--29.5 percentage points higher behavioral fidelity than equally compact rules learned from input words. Further analysis shows that the named concepts reflect internal semantic evidence associated with task decisions, while the learned rules consolidate these distributed signals into inspectable decision structures. The resulting decision knowledge also transfers to lightweight standalone students: using about 1/24 as many parameters as the source classifiers, they reconstruct and execute the representation from raw text while retaining 99.8\% of the source classifiers' mean task accuracy. These findings show that task-specific decision knowledge can be faithfully represented in an explicit, executable form and reused beyond the classifier in which it was learned.

</details>


### [219] [Certified but Private: Scalable Zero-Knowledge Proofs for Neural Network Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17070)
*Youwei Zhong,Ben Merbaum,Timos Antonopoulos,Ning Luo,Charalampos Papamanthou,Katerina Sotiraki,Ruzica Piskac*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PANDA是一个基于零知识证明(ZKP)的可扩展系统，能够在不泄露模型私有参数的情况下，形式化地证明神经网络模型的鲁棒性和公平性。它构建在CROWN鲁棒性认证框架之上，核心创新在于为非线性激活层设计了一种新的线性松弛界证明算法，生成简单轻量的证明。系统可在5分钟内为超过290万参数的神经网络生成局部鲁棒性证明，10秒内完成验证，其复杂度随神经元数量多项式增长，支持比之前方法大4个数量级的网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器学习模型部署日益普遍，在安全关键和合规场景中，对模型鲁棒性和公平性的形式化保证变得越来越重要。然而模型参数通常是商业机密，不能向审计方或终端用户披露，因此需要在保护模型隐私的同时提供可验证的保证。现有基于零知识证明的鲁棒性系统依赖指数级时间算法，无法扩展到有实际意义的网络规模。

Method: PANDA构建在CROWN鲁棒性认证框架之上，核心创新是提出一种新的算法来证明非线性激活层的线性松弛界，从而产生简单轻量的证明。系统利用零知识证明在保护模型私有参数的同时证明鲁棒性和公平性属性，其计算复杂度随网络神经元数量多项式增长，而非指数增长。

Result: PANDA能够为超过290万参数的神经网络在5分钟内生成局部鲁棒性证明，并在10秒内完成验证。相比之前的ZKP鲁棒性方法，PANDA支持的网络规模大了4个数量级，且显著减少了证明者的开销。

Conclusion: PANDA证明了零知识证明可以高效地应用于大规模神经网络的鲁棒性和公平性形式化验证，将可验证的网络规模提升了4个数量级，为在保护模型商业机密的同时满足安全关键和法律合规要求提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: With the growing deployment of machine learning models, formal guarantees of the robustness and fairness of these models have become increasingly important in safety-critical and legal-compliance settings. However, model parameters are often commercial secrets that cannot be disclosed to auditors or end users. To this end, we present PANDA, a scalable system that uses zero-knowledge proofs (ZKPs) to prove the robustness and fairness properties of a model without revealing its private parameters. PANDA is built on top of CROWN, an efficient robustness certification framework that is used in many state-of-the-art formal verification tools for neural networks. The core contribution of PANDA is a novel algorithm for proving linear relaxation bounds for non-linear activation layers, yielding simple, lightweight proofs. Remarkably, our system can generate proofs of local robustness for neural networks with more than 2.9M parameters in 5 minutes, and can verify them in 10 seconds. Prior ZKP-based robustness system rely on exponential-time algorithms that cannot scale to nontrivial networks. In contrast, PANDA scales polynomially in the number of neurons in a network, allowing us to support neural networks 4 orders of magnitude larger than previous approaches with significantly reduced prover overhead.

</details>


### [220] [Dynamic Regime-Aware Conformal Calibration for Reliable Economic Forecast Intervals under Multiple Distribution Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17079)
*Bogdan Oancea*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出动态机制感知共形预测（DRACP），一种用于经济预测的统一加权共形校准框架，结合密度比、局部核和概率机制感知加权与自调优在线显著性控制器。DRACP在48个真实经济序列上与6个基线方法比较，证明其在校准可靠性方面最优（覆盖率最接近名义0.90），但效率不如强自适应在线共形预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共形预测依赖可交换性假设，但在经济预测中常因协变量漂移、概念漂移、局部异质性和潜在机制变化而违反该假设，导致传统方法失效。

Method: DRACP将密度比加权、局部核加权和概率机制感知加权统一到一个加权共形校准框架中，并加入自调优在线显著性控制器。包含三个理论结果：Oracle权重下的有限样本有效性、估计权重的覆盖缺口界限（有效样本尺寸速率）以及在线控制器的确定性或遗憾保证。

Result: DRACP在48个真实序列上评估，覆盖率达到0.890最接近名义0.90，任何序列不低于0.80，所有预测时域保持最佳覆盖，在2021-2023通胀飙升期表现最佳。但强自适应在线共形预测区间窄约20%，效率更高。消融研究显示在线控制器和条件尺度归一化贡献最大。

Conclusion: DRACP在校准可靠性与区间效率之间提供了原则性的权衡，当预测区间必须满足覆盖标准时优先保证可靠覆盖。它不是效率最优方法，但提供了最可靠的校准性能。

Abstract: Conformal prediction provides distribution-free prediction intervals but relies on exchangeability, an assumption often violated in economic forecasting because of covariate shift, concept drift, local heterogeneity and latent regimes. We propose Dynamic Regime-Aware Conformal Prediction (DRACP), which combines density-ratio, localized kernel and probabilistic regime-aware weighting with a self-tuning online significance controller in a unified weighted conformal calibration framework. We distinguish three theoretical results: finite-sample validity under oracle importance weights, a coverage-gap bound for estimated weights with rates in effective sample size, and deterministic or regret guarantees for the online controller. We evaluate DRACP against six baselines on 48 real forecasting series covering euro-area and EU-27 HICP inflation, US macroeconomic and energy indicators, and daily financial series. Recent online methods (FACI, strongly-adaptive online conformal prediction and conformal PID) were verified against the authors' implementations. DRACP is not the most efficient method: strongly-adaptive online conformal prediction achieves the best interval score and intervals about 20% narrower. Instead, DRACP provides the most reliable calibration, achieving coverage closest to the nominal 0.90 (0.890), never falling below 0.80 on any series, maintaining the best coverage at all forecast horizons, and performing best during the 2021-2023 inflation surge. The strongly-adaptive method undercovers on 20 of 48 series versus 10 for DRACP. DRACP therefore offers a principled trade-off between calibration and efficiency, favoring reliable coverage when prediction intervals must satisfy coverage standards. An ablation study shows that the online controller and conditional-scale normalization provide most of the performance gain, whereas the weighting components make a smaller contribution.

</details>


### [221] [Backward through Time, Algebraically](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17087)
*Konstantinos Kogkalidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个代数泛型（algebra-generic）的可微分的线性时序逻辑（LTL）评估引擎，将读者置于函数式程序员视角，拒绝过早承诺单一语义代数。该引擎附带可执行规范，并实现了多种代数，每种代数代表一种"失望方向"的选择。全部内容以PyTorch库telos形式开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性时序逻辑（LTL）在布尔域上是规范的，但对软值系统（神经策略、自适应控制器、序列模型等）的离散化判断难以作为训练信号。目标公式的（不）满足程度需要成为可微分的训练信号，但现有的可微语义众多且难以导航；已有的实现多为浅嵌入（shallow embedding），要求用户提前承诺单一的语义代数及其隐含行为。作者拒绝这种预先承诺，试图解决这一困境。

Method: 作者以一个拒绝接受困境的函数式程序员视角，构建了一个代数泛型的评估引擎，该引擎不受特定语义代数的束缚，同时支持微分。论文还提供一个可执行规范来形式化引擎可接受的代数集合。针对多种代数进行了前向和后向行为审计，每种代数被揭示为"选择在哪个方向失望以及如何失望"。

Result: 实现了多种可微语义代数，并对它们的前向与反向传播行为进行了审计。结果表明每种代数本质上代表了对"如何以及在哪个方向上产生失望信号"的一种选择。所有实现（及更多内容）打包为PyTorch库telos，并开源在GitHub上。

Conclusion: 该工作通过代数泛型设计，将可微LTL语义的选择从"实现时的隐含承诺"提升为"显式可审计的配置"，使不同的失望策略可以灵活切换。这为软值系统的时序约束训练提供了更透明、更可组合的工具链。

Abstract: Linear temporal logic is a modal extension of propositional logic that allows one to state how a system should behave over time. Its canonical domain is the booleans, but discretely-valued judgements are of little use in steering softly-valued systems (neural policies, adaptive controllers, sequence models, etc). In such cases, the goal formula's (dis)satisfaction becomes a training signal, and differentiability becomes a prime concern. Candidate differentiable semantics abound, but navigating them is tricky. Implementations, where available, are shallow embeddings, demanding an upfront commitment to a single semantic algebra and its (usually implicit) conduct. The paper casts the reader as a functional programmer asked to come to terms with this predicament, and refusing. Out of that refusal comes an evaluation engine that is algebra-generic and amenable to differentiation, together with an executable specification of the algebras it can accept. Various algebras are implemented and audited for their behavior, both forward and backward. Each algebra turns out to be a choice of which direction to disappoint, and how. Everything described (and more) is part of the PyTorch library telos, to be found at https://github.com/konstantinosKokos/telos.

</details>


### [222] [Deep Learning for Cross-Border Electricity Price Forecasting: A Comparative Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17091)
*Hadeer Elashhab,Sai Srijan Papineni,Marvin Dorn,Veit Hagenmeyer,Benjamin Schäfer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用公开的电力市场数据，建立了一个可复现的基准框架，在德国-卢森堡（DE-LU）2024年投标区域，对六种深度学习模型（涵盖状态空间、MLP、RNN和Transformer架构）进行电力价格预测（EPF）的跨市场泛化比较评估。通过零样本、单样本和少样本学习模拟低数据目标市场条件，研究发现N-HiTS和NBEATSx在有限数据场景下表现优异，而Transformer模型虽可达到相当精度但需要更多调优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电力市场预测研究领域缺乏统一的基准数据集和标准化评估方法，导致不同研究使用不同数据集与指标，难以公平比较各方法的进展和先进水平。

Method: 使用公开数据构建标准化数据集（包含日历、历史价格和市场衍生特征），在DE-LU 2024年投标区域上对六种深度学习模型进行对比评估，并采用零样本、一样本和少样本学习模拟低数据目标市场条件以检验模型的跨市场泛化能力。

Result: N-HiTS和NBEATSx在有限数据场景下表现具有竞争力；Transformer模型可达到相当的精度但需要更多适应和调优；特征选择和超参数调优对模型性能有显著影响；最强模型之间的性能差异通常较小。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一个可复现的电力价格预测基准框架，有助于标准化评估和比较不同深度学习模型，并揭示了在低数据场景下不同架构的优势与局限。

Abstract: While publicly available electricity market data presents a valuable resource for forecasting research, the field lacks established benchmark datasets for standardized comparison. As a result, many studies have relied on different datasets and metrics to evaluate methods in isolated settings, making it difficult to assess progress and compare state-of-the-art approaches consistently. In this work, we use public data to evaluate deep learning models for electricity price forecasting (EPF) across multiple market settings. Our goal is to establish a reproducible framework that enables a consistent evaluation of forecasting models. Although deep learning has been explored for day-ahead EPF, many prior studies are limited to single-market settings, narrow feature sets, or fixed training regimes. This work presents a comparative evaluation of six deep learning models--covering state-space, MLP, RNN, and Transformer-based architectures--emphasizing generalization across markets. We simulate low-data target-market conditions using zero-shot, one-shot, and few-shot learning. Our test set focuses on the Germany-Luxembourg (DE-LU) bidding zone in 2024 using a standardized dataset with calendar, historical price, and market-derived features. Our findings suggest that N-HiTS and NBEATSx perform competitively in limited-data scenarios, while transformer-based models can reach comparable accuracy but tend to require more adaptation and tuning. Model performance also benefits from careful feature selection and hyperparameter tuning, and we note that the differences between the strongest models are often small.

</details>


### [223] [From Abductive Explanations to Global Logical Rules for Node Classification in SGCs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17103)
*Bryan Lima Cavalcante,Thiago Alves Rocha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于逻辑的框架，用于Simple Graph Convolution (SGC)网络的节点分类解释。该方法为每个节点计算最小反事实解释（最小节点-特征对集合），用于训练决策树并提取全局逻辑规则。实验表明该方法能生成紧凑的全局规则，同时保持对原始SGC模型的高保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有逻辑方法（如LogicXGNN）从解释性子图中提取全局逻辑规则，但这些子图可能包含特定于单个节点的冗余结构信息，限制了所提取规则的泛化性。因此需要更简洁、更具通用性的中间表示来提取全局规则。

Method: 1) 对每个节点计算最小反事实解释（minimal abductive explanations），即足以保持预测类别的最小节点-特征对集合；2) 将这些解释作为中间表示用于训练决策树；3) 从决策树中提取全局逻辑规则。

Result: 在基准数据集上的实验表明，该框架能够生成紧凑的全局规则，同时保持对原始SGC模型的高保真度。

Conclusion: 使用最小反事实解释作为中间表示，可以有效减少冗余结构信息，生成更紧凑且更通用的全局逻辑规则，同时保持对模型预测的高度解释准确性。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have achieved remarkable performance in node classification tasks, motivating growing interest in methods capable of explaining their predictions. Recent logic-based approaches, such as LogicXGNN, derive global logical rules for Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) from collections of explanatory subgraphs. While informative, these subgraphs may contain redundant structural information that is specific to individual nodes, potentially limiting the generality of the extracted rules. In this work, we propose a logic-based framework for node classification in Simple Graph Convolution (SGC) networks that uses minimal abductive explanations as an intermediate representation for rule extraction. For each node, we compute a minimal set of node-feature pairs sufficient to preserve the predicted class. These explanations are then used to train decision trees from which global logical rules are extracted. Experiments on benchmark datasets show that the proposed framework produces compact global rules while maintaining high fidelity to the original SGC model.

</details>


### [224] [Causal Discovery in Equal Variance Linear Gaussian DAGs via SURE-Tuned Ridge Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17132)
*Sambit Mishra,Urbashi Mitra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SURE-Ridge，一种非迭代、闭式解的估计器，用于等方差线性高斯结构方程模型的DAG恢复。该方法通过Stein无偏风险估计自适应选择正则化参数进行并行节点回归，并采用自适应阈值化从软邻接矩阵中提取DAG，在小样本和计算受限场景中优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的连续优化方法（如NOTEARS）依赖迭代梯度下降和逐问题的超参数调优，难以应对两种重要场景：样本受限（样本数与节点数相当或更少）和计算受限。需要一种非迭代、无需繁琐调参的替代方案。

Method: SURE-Ridge采用三个关键步骤：(1) 并行节点回归，利用Ridge回归处理每个节点的预测；(2) 通过Stein无偏风险估计（SURE）自适应选择正则化参数，避免人工调参；(3) 对得到的软邻接矩阵应用自适应阈值化操作以提取最终的DAG结构。

Result: 数值实验表明，与NOTEARS、DAGMA和GBNSL基线相比，SURE-Ridge在小样本场景下取得最低的结构汉明距离（SHD），并且在所有测试样本量下运行时间最短。

Conclusion: SURE-Ridge为非迭代的DAG恢复提供了有效方案，特别适用于小样本和计算受限场景，兼顾结构恢复精度和计算效率。

Abstract: Recovering the directed acyclic graph (DAG) of a structural equation model (SEM) from observational data is a central problem in causal discovery. The iterative gradient descent and per-problem hyperparameter tuning of continuous-optimization methods are poorly suited to two practically important regimes: the sample-limited regime, where the number of samples is comparable to or smaller than the number of nodes in the DAG, and the compute-limited regime. This work proposes SURE-Ridge, a non-iterative, closed-form estimator for equal variance linear Gaussian SEM. The method performs parallel node-wise regressions with regularization parameters chosen adaptively by Stein's unbiased risk estimate (SURE), and applies an adaptive thresholding procedure to extract a DAG from the resulting soft adjacency matrix. Numerical results show that SURE-Ridge achieves the lowest structural Hamming distance in the small-sample regime and the lowest run time across all sample sizes tested, compared with NOTEARS, DAGMA, and GBNSL baselines.

</details>


### [225] [Iterative tensor network transformations for element-wise evaluation of elementary and filtering functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17135)
*Xiao Wang,Tomohiro Hashizume,Pia Siegl,Dieter Jaksch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出迭代张量网络变换（ITNTs），一种在压缩域中对张量训练（TT）格式编码的数据执行非线性初等函数和滤波函数逐元素计算的通用算法框架，可高效处理指数级规模的数据集，并展示了其在3D反应流场计算和Max-SAT优化（高达2^70配置空间）中的强大能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 张量网络是压缩大规模数据的强大工具，但其在通用数据处理中的应用受到非线性运算困难的限制，需要一种能够在压缩域中执行非线性操作的通用算法框架。

Method: 提出迭代张量网络变换（ITNTs）算法框架，完全在压缩域中对编码为张量训练（TT）格式的数据进行逐元素非线性函数和滤波函数的评估，通过迭代方式保持计算成本可控。

Result: 在两个关键领域展示了有效性：(I) 在3D反应流场上评估高度非线性的初等函数和滤波函数，实现高保真反应速率计算和区域滤波；(II) 在复杂优化问题中寻找极值，成功解决配置空间高达2^70的Max-SAT实例。

Conclusion: ITNT为张量网络方法提供了通用数据科学和大规模优化的基础能力，是一个基础性工具，拓展了张量网络的适用范围。

Abstract: Tensor networks are powerful formats for compressing large-scale data. However, their application to general data processing has been limited by the difficulty of performing nonlinear operations. Here, we introduce iterative tensor network transformations (ITNTs), a general algorithmic framework for the element-wise evaluation of elementary and nonlinear filtering functions on data encoded as tensor trains (TTs), a class of tensor networks. Our approach operates entirely in the compressed domain, enabling efficient computation on exponentially large datasets while maintaining a controlled computational cost. We demonstrate its power in two key areas: (I) evaluating highly nonlinear elementary and filtering functions on a 3D reactive flow field, enabling high-fidelity reaction rate computation and region filtering, and (II) finding extrema in complex optimization problems, such as solving Max-SAT instances on spaces up to $2^{70}$ configurations. These results establish ITNT as a foundational tool that provides tensor network methods with the capability for general-purpose data science and large-scale optimization.

</details>


### [226] [OraclePhys: A Systematic Framework for LLM Fine-Tuning on Structural Mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17162)
*Mingyu Li,Guorui Song,Jing Lin,Haoqian Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OraclePhys，一个系统性的微调框架，用于研究语言模型从微调中内化什么。该框架包含三个组件：OraclePhys-Bench（基于有限元oracle的精确评分结构力学基准）、OraclePhys-30K（七种答案形式的监督数据集）以及覆盖七种形式和三种验证器角色的受控训练研究。研究发现：标签的答案形式（而非比特数）因果性地决定微调所教的内容——排序目标能安装分布外前向模型，标量目标只能部分安装，布尔目标无可检测效果；且GRPO提升奖励但模型在保留物理数据上统计等效于起点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型从微调中内化了什么通常只能在事后诊断，本文希望将此作为实验变量进行系统性研究，以理解微调数据的标签形式对模型能力习得的因果影响。

Method: 构建OraclePhys框架，包含精确评分的结构力学基准（OraclePhys-Bench，使用有限元oracle评分）、七种答案形式的监督数据集（OraclePhys-30K），以及在七种形式和三种验证器角色间进行受控训练研究。

Result: 排序目标安装分布外前向模型（未训练基座处于猜测先验），标量目标最多部分安装，布尔目标无可检测效果；向量-标量差距在第二物理领域、第二模型家族和释义评估表面下依然存在。书面或分数过滤答案能安装此能力，而GRPO虽提高奖励但模型在保留物理测试上统计等效于起点。训练的8B模型达到任务数据精度前沿：超过前沿LLM在零样本和32样本的表现，达到专家水平。

Conclusion: 标签对目标计算的明确程度决定了微调所教的内容；训练数据的内容决定了模型的路由能力。标签的答案形式因果性地决定微调传授什么能力，而非数据量或比特数。

Abstract: What a language model internalizes from fine-tuning is usually diagnosed after the fact. We make it an experimental variable. OraclePhys is a systematic fine-tuning framework with three components: OraclePhys-Bench, an exactly-graded structural-mechanics benchmark whose finite-element oracle scores every answer and counterfactual edit -- no human labels, no LLM judging; OraclePhys-30K, a supervision dataset of seven answer forms over byte-identical structure descriptions; and a controlled training study across the seven forms and three verifier roles. The study yields two findings. First, the label's answer form -- not its bit count -- causally determines what fine-tuning teaches: a ranking objective installs an out-of-distribution forward model where the untrained base sits at the guessing prior, a scalar objective at best a partial one, a boolean nothing detectable; the vector-scalar gulf survives a second physics domain, a second model family, and a paraphrased evaluation surface. Second, written or score-filtered answers install this capability, while advantage-weighted scores (GRPO) raise reward yet leave the model statistically equivalent to its start on held-out physics -- within the recipes and budgets tested -- sufficing only for routing. The trained 8B -- the first LLM on spatial structural response -- reaches the task's data-precision frontier: above a frontier LLM at zero- and 32-shot, at a specialist's level. What the label spells out about the target computation is what fine-tuning teaches; what you train on is what you route.

</details>


### [227] [Q-Learning With World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17163)
*Perry Dong,Yueru Jia,Chelsea Finn,Dorsa Sadigh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QWM框架将世界模型与标准Q-learning结合，在测试时通过想象轨迹搜索来选择高价值动作，同时保持策略和价值函数仅基于真实转移训练，避免了复合模型偏差，在Robomimic和LIBERO操作基准上显著提升了样本效率和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于模型RL方法直接在想象回滚上优化策略或价值函数，容易产生复合偏差，难以扩展到高维真实机器人问题。作者希望在不引入这种偏差的前提下，利用世界模型提升标准Q-learning的性能。

Method: 提出QWM框架，在世界模型之上进行测试时搜索，对想象轨迹进行评估以选择高价值动作，应用于在线回滚和评估阶段。核心在于策略和价值函数仅用真实转移训练，世界模型只用于预测搜索，从而实现两者解耦。

Result: 在Robomimic和LIBERO挑战性操作基准上，QWM在样本效率和最终性能两方面均显著优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过将世界模型仅用于测试时搜索而非策略训练，可以有效提升策略学习性能，同时规避基于模型方法固有的复合偏差问题，为世界模型在大规模高维机器人学习中的应用提供了新路径。

Abstract: Off-policy reinforcement learning (RL) has become increasingly sample-efficient, enabling applications such as RL fine-tuning of Vision-Language-Action models into reliable, high-performing policies. World models offer a further lever for sample efficiency, as they predict state changes rather than actions alone, but their success has largely been confined to supervised policy learning. Prior model-based RL methods often optimize the policy or value function directly on imagined rollouts, which is prone to compounding bias and struggles to scale to large, high-dimensional problems such as real-world robotics, a problem that worsens with task horizon and visual complexity. In this work, we instead ask whether we can leverage world models directly on top of standard Q-learning to improve performance, while remaining trained and grounded in the real, online setting. We propose QWM, a framework that leverages world models to perform test-time search over imagined trajectories on top of Q-learning to select high-value actions during both online rollouts and evaluation. Since the policy and value function are trained only on real transitions, QWM avoids compounding model bias while still gaining the sample-efficiency benefits of predictive search. On challenging manipulation benchmarks Robomimic and LIBERO, QWM significantly outperforms strong prior state-of-the-art methods on both sample efficiency and performance.

</details>


### [228] [SCENARIODIFF: A Scenario-level Guidance Framework for Multimodal Time Series Forecasting--Extended Version](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17164)
*Tuan-Binh Tran,Dat Nguyen Cong,Duc-Trong Le,Thanh Trung Huynh,Tung Kieu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SCENARIODIFF提出了一种层次化情境推理框架，用于多模态时间序列预测。它将上下文组织为三个层次：历史上下文智能体提取逐步证据、场景智能体生成定性场景描述、锚点引导智能体生成稀疏锚点，并通过条件化的多模态扩散Transformer和锚点混合采样进行预测，在Time-MMD基准上展现了事件驱动领域的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态时间序列预测方法要么让LLM直接预测数值，要么隐式融合文本与时间序列，导致情境影响难以解释和控制。尤其在事件驱动的场景中，外部事件尚未在历史数值中显现，需要显式的情境引导来提高预测的可解释性和可控性。

Method: SCENARIODIFF采用三层次层次化情境推理：(1) 历史上下文智能体从原始文档中提取逐步证据；(2) 场景智能体为预测区间生成定性场景描述；(3) 锚点引导智能体为事件相关未来区域生成稀疏锚点。这些结构化信号条件化多模态扩散Transformer，并通过锚点混合采样在不需要重新训练的情况下局部优化生成轨迹。

Result: 在Time-MMD基准上的实验表明，SCENARIODIFF在事件驱动领域尤为有效，验证了显式层次化场景引导对多模态时间序列预测的价值。

Conclusion: 显式的层次化场景引导（历史证据→场景描述→锚点）能够有效提升多模态时间序列预测的性能，特别是在噪声大、弱对齐文档和事件驱动场景下，该方法具有明显的优势。

Abstract: Textual context such as news, reports, and logs can provide valuable signals for time series forecasting, especially when future dynamics are driven by external events that are not yet visible in historical values. Existing multimodal forecasting methods often either ask large language models (LLMs) to predict numerical values directly or fuse text and time series implicitly, making contextual influence difficult to interpret and control. We propose SCENARIODIFF, a hierarchical contextual reasoning framework for multimodal time series forecasting under noisy and weakly aligned documents. SCENARIODIFF organizes contextual information into three levels: a Historical Context Agent extracts stepwise evidence from raw documents, a Scenario Agent produces a qualitative scenario description for the forecast horizon, and an Anchor Guidance Agent generates sparse anchor points for event-relevant future regions. These structured signals condition a Multimodal Diffusion Transformer, while Anchor Blended Sampling locally refines generated trajectories without retraining. Experiments on the Time-MMD benchmark show that SCENARIODIFF is especially effective in event-driven domains, demonstrating the value of explicit hierarchical scenario guidance for multimodal time series forecasting. Our full implementation is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/ScenarioDiff_ICDM-2C4C

</details>


### [229] [Population Health-Based Machine Learning Reveals Associations Between Psychosocial Factors and Chronic Kidney Disease](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17174)
*Md. Atik Shams,David Eisenberg,Sumaiya Fatema,Asma Sultana,D. M Hasibul Islam,Junnatul Mawa,Anindita Datta,Nafiya Ahmed,Danastan Tasaouf Mridula,SK. Sazid Mahmud,Simon Bin Akter,Tanjila Helaly,Jorge Fresneda Fernandez,Humayera Islam,Tanmoy Sarkar Pias*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用大规模远程医疗数据（BRFSS与NHIS数据集）结合机器学习方法，构建自定义堆叠集成模型对慢性肾病（CKD）进行自报告状态分类，并通过SHAP分析识别关键疾病驱动因素。模型平衡准确率达到72.56-76.12%，AUROC为79.59-82.29%，揭示了常规体检、年龄、血压和心理健康压力等关键预测因子，为CKD风险分层提供了稳健且可解释的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 慢性肾病（CKD）进展隐匿且严重损害生活质量，早期检测对改善患者预后至关重要。然而现有检测手段往往在疾病已进展后才被发现，因此亟需利用大规模数据与先进机器学习方法实现早期识别与风险分层。

Method: 采用两阶段研究设计：(1) 使用BRFSS 2021/2019和NHIS 2021/2020四个大规模数据集，共约91.8万样本；(2) 运用九种先进插补方法处理缺失数据，通过采样策略缓解类别不平衡问题；(3) 构建自定义堆叠集成模型进行分类；(4) 结合SHAP分析与临床审查识别关键预测因子。

Result: 堆叠集成模型实现平衡准确率72.56-76.12%，AUROC分数为79.59-82.29%。SHAP分析结合临床审查确定了常规体检、年龄、血压及心理健康压力指标为CKD的关键预测因素。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一个稳健且可解释的CKD风险分层框架，不仅能准确分类CKD状态，还提供了与疾病相关的可操作因素洞见，对CKD早期检测和干预具有重要临床实用价值。

Abstract: Chronic kidney disease (CKD) progresses silently and severely undermines quality of life, making early detection critical for improving patient outcomes. We present a two-part study that combines large-scale telehealth data with advanced machine learning to both classify self-reported CKD status and identify key drivers of disease. Using selected features from the Behavioral Risk Factor Surveillance System (BRFSS 2021: 438,693 samples; BRFSS 2019: 418,268 samples) and the National Health Interview Survey (NHIS 2021: 29,482 samples; NHIS 2020: 31,568 samples), we addressed missing data with nine state-of-the-art imputation methods and mitigated class imbalance via sampling strategies. Our customized stacked ensemble model achieved balanced accuracy of 72.56-76.12%, with corresponding AUROC scores of 79.59-82.29%. SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP) analysis, followed by clinical review, highlighted critical predictors, including regular medical check-ups, age, blood pressure, and indicators of mental health stress. These findings deliver a robust and interpretable framework for CKD risk stratification and provide actionable insights into its associated factors.

</details>


### [230] [Task Specialization Fine-Tuning for Contextual Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17180)
*Jianan Zhou,Jung-Hoon Cho,Tianyue Zhou,Han Zheng,Jie Zhang,Roy Dong,Yining Ma,Cathy Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TSFT框架，在预训练策略基础上通过整数线性规划精确求解预算分配问题，实现样本高效的任务特化微调，在组合优化、连续控制和LLM微调等多个领域显著提升任务覆盖度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CRL方法通常从零训练，依赖多任务学习单一策略或策略性训练多个策略，但忽略了现代预训练-微调范式的潜力。在预训练策略和有限预算条件下，如何为不同任务区域分配微调资源以实现样本高效的CRL是一个关键且未解决的问题。

Method: 提出Task Specialization Fine-Tuning (TSFT)在线框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 使用简单参数化模型预测各任务区域的微调性能；(2) 将离散预算分配问题建模为整数线性规划并精确求解，从而决定每个任务区域应获得的微调资源量。

Result: 在组合优化、连续控制和LLM微调等多种决策领域的广泛实验中，TSFT在任务覆盖度上显著优于基线方法，并接近Oracle性能。

Conclusion: 该方法为基于模型的CRL开辟了新方向，与预训练-微调时代相契合，证明通过智能预算分配可以实现样本高效的CRL。

Abstract: Contextual Reinforcement Learning (CRL) seeks to generalize classical RL by maximizing task coverage across a context space of related tasks. While prior works often train from scratch and rely on either multi-task learning for a single policy or strategically training multiple policies, we advocate for a unified alternative: pretraining a single policy with good initial performance, followed by fine-tuning multiple policies for task specialization. This new paradigm, however, introduces unique challenges, such as heterogeneous marginal returns and sample inefficiency. This raises a critical research question: given a pretrained policy and a constrained budget, how much fine-tuning should each task region receive to enable sample-efficient CRL? To this end, we propose Task Specialization Fine-Tuning (TSFT), an online framework that predicts fine-tuning performance with a simple parametric model and exactly solves the resulting discrete budget allocation problem via integer linear programming. Extensive experiments across diverse decision domains, including combinatorial optimization, continuous control, and LLM fine-tuning, demonstrate that TSFT significantly outperforms baselines in task coverage and approaches oracle performance. Our work charts a new direction for model-based CRL, aligning with the modern pretrain-finetune era.

</details>


### [231] [Reinforcement Learning as (Discrete) Potential Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17181)
*Christopher Connolly*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了概率论与位势理论（potential theory）之间的联系，并从位势理论的视角重新审视了固定策略下的核心强化学习表示与算法，指出这一视角可能为提升样本效率和施加形式化约束提供新路径，并可将线性框架自然地推广到非线性情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习理论基于马尔可夫链，而马尔可夫链本质上是概率论的研究对象。作者指出概率论与位势理论之间存在深层联系，因此希望通过引入位势理论的视角来为强化学习提供新的理论工具，从而改善样本效率并施加形式化约束。

Method: 本文是一篇综述性论文，系统回顾了概率论与位势理论之间的连接，并以此为基础重新审视固定策略假设下的核心强化学习表示（如价值函数、策略评估等）和算法。进一步地，作者探讨了当固定策略假设被放宽时，线性位势理论框架如何自然延伸到非线性情形。

Result: 论文表明位势理论视角可以为强化学习提供新的解释框架，可能为改进样本效率以及施加形式化约束提供理论路径，并且该框架可以自然地扩展到非线性情形。

Conclusion: 位势理论为强化学习提供了有前景的新视角，有望在样本效率和形式化约束方面带来进展，其扩展性也使其适用于更广泛的非固定策略场景。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) theory fundamentally depends on probability theory through the Markov chain. There is a deep connection between probability theory and potential theory. This paper reviews that connection and explores the potential-theoretic viewpoint for core reinforcement learning representations and algorithms under a fixed-policy assumption. This viewpoint may offer a path for improved sample efficiency and formal constraints that can be applied to RL. When the fixed-policy assumption is relaxed, the linear potential theory framework can be naturally extended to the nonlinear case.

</details>


### [232] [How smoothing the affinity matrix affects neighborhood preservation in t-SNE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17190)
*Shirin Mohebi,Guillaume Bied,Jefrey Lijffijt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究t-SNE中亲和矩阵概率分布的锐度对邻域保持的影响，提出一种由参数gamma控制的行向幂变换来平滑或锐化亲和矩阵的每一行，该变换等价于重缩放高斯带宽从而改变困惑度，但由于逐点不同的有效困惑度，它不同于全局改变困惑度。实验表明锐化改善最近邻保持，平滑改善更广泛局部邻域的保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: t-SNE是广泛使用的降维可视化方法，其核心是亲和矩阵所表达的成对相似性概率分布。然而，该概率分布的锐度如何影响不同尺度下的邻域保持尚未被系统研究。

Method: 引入一个由参数gamma控制的行向幂变换，可平滑或锐化亲和矩阵的每一行，同时保持稀疏性和秩序。理论上证明该变换等价于重缩放高斯带宽（即改变困惑度），但固定gamma会导致逐点相关的有效困惑度，使其区别于全局改变困惑度。通过实验对比不同gamma设置及多种亲和构造方法（包括多尺度方法）的邻域保持效果。

Result: 实验发现锐化（gamma>1）可改善最近邻的保持，而平滑（gamma<1）可改善更广泛局部邻域的保持；在中间局部范围内，该方法优于包括多尺度方法在内的其他亲和构造方法。

Conclusion: t-SNE中亲和矩阵的锐度是一个可调且有效的控制维度，通过gamma参数可以灵活地在不同尺度上权衡邻域保持质量，为改进t-SNE可视化提供了新的调节手段。
